var GDPR = {
	consentCookie: "user_consent",
	GA_DISABLED: "GA_DISABLED",
	GA_DISABLED_2: "GA_DISABLED_2",
	disabled_tracking_key: "web_tracking_disabled",

	respect: function(){
		//document this!
		if(sessionStorage.getItem(GDPR.GA_DISABLED)){
			window[sessionStorage.getItem(GDPR.GA_DISABLED)] = true;
			if(sessionStorage.getItem(GDPR.GA_DISABLED_2)){
				window[sessionStorage.getItem(GDPR.GA_DISABLED_2)] = true;
			}
		}
		if(sessionStorage.getItem(GDPR.disabled_tracking_key)){
			return;
		}

        /*
		if(window.navigator.doNotTrack === "1") { //changed this to === "1"
			GDPR.disableForNHSTDomains();
			return;
		}
        */

		if(GDPR.isCookiePresent(GDPR.consentCookie)){
			var settings = JSON.parse(atob(GDPR.getCookie(GDPR.consentCookie).replace('\"', "").replace('\"', '')));
			if(settings.disableTracking === true){
				GDPR.disableForNHSTDomains();
                return;
			}
		}
        GDPR.enableTracking();
	},

	disableForNHSTDomains: function(){
		var domain = GDPR.getDomainName().toLowerCase();
		switch(domain) {
					case "dn.no":
						GDPR.disableForDN();
						break;
					case "tradewindsnews.com":
						GDPR.disableForGlobals(domain);
						break;
					case "rechargenews.com":
						GDPR.disableForGlobals(domain);
						break;
					case "upstreamonline.com":
						GDPR.disableForGlobals(domain);
						break;
					case "intrafish.com":
						GDPR.disableForGlobals(domain);
						break;
					case "intrafish.no":
						GDPR.disableForGlobals(domain);
						break;
					case "fiskeribladet.no":
						GDPR.disableForFiskeribladet();
						break;
					case "europower.com":
            			GDPR.disableForEnergiNett();
						GDPR.disableForEuropower(true);
						break;
          			case "energi-nett.no":
            			GDPR.disableForEuropower();
						GDPR.disableForEnergiNett(true);
						break;
					case "morgenbladet.no":
						GDPR.disableForMorgenbladet();
						break;
					default:
						console.log("unknown domain");
						break;
				}
				sessionStorage.setItem(GDPR.disabled_tracking_key, true);
	},

	enableTracking: function(){
        if (GDPR.getDomainName().toLowerCase() != "dn.no") {
            var expires = "Thu, 01 Jan 1970 00:00:01 GMT";
            GDPR.setCookie("__hs_opt_out","",expires)
            GDPR.setCookie("__hstc","","",expires);
            GDPR.setCookie("hubspotutk","","",expires);
            GDPR.setCookie("__hssc","","",expires);
            GDPR.setCookie("__hssrc","","",expires);
        }
        
        GDPR.setCookie('sat_track', 'true',GDPR.getDateAYearFromNow().toGMTString());
	},

	isCookiePresent: function(cookieName){
		var cookies = document.cookie.split('; ');
		var regex = new RegExp(''+cookieName+'=.+', 'g' );
		var found = false;
		for(var i = 0; i < cookies.length;i++){
			if(cookies[i].match(regex) != null){
				found = true;
				break;
			}
		}
		return found;
	},
	disableForDN: function(){
         // Added functionality to disable for both GA accounts for DN 
        var tid1 = 'UA-3199200-1';
        var tid2 = 'UA-3199200-22';
        GDPR.disableGoogleTracking(tid1);
        GDPR.disableGoogleTrackingSecondary(tid2);

	},

    disableForMorgenbladet: function(){

	disableForGlobals: function(domain){
		var GA_ID;
		switch(domain) {
				case "tradewindsnews.com":
					GA_ID = "UA-3357685-1";
					break;
				case "rechargenews.com":
					GA_ID = "UA-5635124-1";
					break;
				case "upstreamonline.com":
					GA_ID = "UA-4676970-1";

					break;
				case "intrafish.no":

					break;
		}
		GDPR.disableGoogleTracking(GA_ID);
		GDPR.disableAdobeTracking();
		GDPR.disableHubspotTracking();
		GDPR.disableOntheTracking();
	},
	
	getCookie: function(name) {
		var value = "; " + document.cookie;
		var parts = value.split("; " + name + "=");
		if (parts.length == 2) return parts.pop().split(";").shift();
	},

	setCookie: function(cookieName, cookieValue, expires){
		var domain = GDPR.getDomainName().toLowerCase();
		var cookieDomain = "."+domain;
		var cookiestring = cookieName +"=" + cookieValue + ";expires=" + expires + ";domain="+cookieDomain+";path=/";
		console.log(cookiestring);
		document.cookie = cookiestring;
	},

	disableAdobeTracking: function(){
		var expires = GDPR.getDateAYearFromNow().toGMTString();
		console.log("setting adobe sat_track cookie");
		GDPR.setCookie('sat_track', 'false',expires);
	},

	disableGoogleTracking: function(id){
		sessionStorage.setItem(GDPR.GA_DISABLED, 'ga-disable-'+id);
		window['ga-disable-'+id] = true;
	},

	disableGoogleTrackingSecondary: function(id){
		sessionStorage.setItem(GDPR.GA_DISABLED_2, 'ga-disable-'+id);
		window['ga-disable-'+id] = true;
	},

	},

	},

	getDateAYearFromNow: function(){
		var cookieDate = new Date();
		cookieDate.setFullYear(cookieDate.getFullYear( )+1);
		return cookieDate;
	},
	
	getDomainName: function(){
		var hostName = window.location.hostname;
	    hostName =  hostName.substring(hostName.lastIndexOf(".", hostName.lastIndexOf(".") - 1) + 1);
	    return hostName.toLowerCase();
	},

22%5B%5D%22%2C%22browserSize%22%3A%22%5B600%2C%200%5D%22%7D%2C%7B%22slotSize%22%3A%22%5B%5D%22%2C%22browserSize%22%3A%22%5B0%2C%200%5D%22%7D%5D%7D%2C%22googlead-sem-johnsen-profier-frontbanner-2%22%3A%7B%22desktopSize%22%3A%22100%25xauto%22%2C%22showOnTablet%22%3Atrue%2C%22showOnMobile%22%3Atrue%2C%22tabletSize%22%3A%22100%25xauto%22%2C%22title%22%3A%22ANNONSE-Sem%20%26%20Johnsen%20%2B%20Profier%20Frontbanner%202%22%2C%22type%22%3A%22sphynx-ad%22%2C%22protected%22%3Atrue%2C%22size%22%3A%22%5B%5B2%2C%202%5D%2C%20%5B300%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C265%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20320%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20400%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20480%5D%2C%20%5B336%2C%20280%5D%2C%20%5B580%2C%20400%5D%2C%20%5B580%2C%20500%5D%2C%20%5B728%2C%2090%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20299%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20300%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C600%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20650%5D%5D%22%2C%22desktopGrid%22%3A%22full%22%2C%22mobileSize%22%3A%22300x250%22%2C%22id%22%3A%22googlead-sem-johnsen-profier-frontbanner-2%22%2C%22placeholder%22%3A%22lantern_placeholder_ad%22%2C%22showOnDesktop%22%3Atrue%2C%22slotUrl%22%3A%22%2F21646926696%2Fdn.no%2Ffrontbanner_eiendom%22%2C%22pixelDistance%22%3A200%2C%22sizeMapping%22%3A%5B%7B%22slotSize%22%3A%22%5B%5B2%2C%202%5D%2C%20%5B970%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20299%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20300%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20600%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20650%5D%5D%22%2C%22browserSize%22%3A%22%5B900%2C%200%5D%22%7D%2C%7B%22slotSize%22%3A%22%5B%5B2%2C%202%5D%2C%20%5B580%2C%20400%5D%2C%20%5B580%2C%20500%5D%2C%20%5B728%2C%2090%5D%2C%20%5B980%2C%20299%5D%5D%22%2C%22browserSize%22%3A%22%5B600%2C%200%5D%22%7D%2C%7B%22slotSize%22%3A%22%5B%5B2%2C%202%5D%2C%20%5B300%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C265%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20320%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20400%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20480%5D%2C%20%5B336%2C%20280%5D%5D%22%2C%22browserSize%22%3A%22%5B320%2C%200%5D%22%7D%2C%7B%22slotSize%22%3A%22%5B%5B2%2C%202%5D%2C%20%5B300%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20250%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C265%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20320%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20400%5D%2C%20%5B320%2C%20480%5D%2C%20%5B336%2C%20280%5D%5D%22%2C%22browserSize%22%3A%22%5B0%2C0%5D%22%7D%5D%7D%7D";
    var ads = {};
    var slots = {};
    var adPos = 0;
    var cookieNotificationStatus = window.nhst.cookieMessageShown ? 'Lukk' : '';
  
    var isValid = function (placeholder) {
      if (!placeholder.disabled && placeholder.slotUrl !== ''
        && typeof placeholder.size !== '')
        return true;
      return false;
    };
  
    var parseVal = function (val) {
      var value = val;
      try {
        value = JSON.parse(val);
      } catch (e) {
      }
      return value;
    };
  
    if (typeof placeholders !== 'undefined') {
  
      try {
        ads = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(placeholders));
      } catch (e) {
      }
  
      //if(!(Object.keys(ads).length === 0 && ads.constructor === Object)) {
      googletag.cmd.push(function () {
        $.each(ads, function (i, value) {
  
          if (value.type === 'sphynx-ad' && isValid(value)) {
  
            var slot = googletag.defineSlot(value.slotUrl, parseVal(value.size), value.id)
              .addService(googletag.pubads());
  
            if (value.sizeMapping.length > 0 && value.sizeMapping[0] !== null) {
              var mappingBuilder = googletag.sizeMapping();
              value.sizeMapping.forEach(function (element) {
                mappingBuilder.addSize(parseVal(element.browserSize), parseVal(element.slotSize));
              });
              slot.defineSizeMapping(mappingBuilder.build());
            }
            slots[value.id] = slot;
          }
        });
  
        googletag.pubads().setTargeting('mode', 'prod');
        googletag.pubads().setTargeting('dn_loginstatus', loginstatus);
        googletag.pubads().setTargeting('dn_section', ["forsiden"])
        googletag.pubads().setTargeting("dn_topic", [])
        googletag.pubads().setTargeting("dn_keyword", []);
        googletag.pubads().setTargeting("dn_cookieinfo", cookieNotificationStatus);
  
        googletag.pubads().enableSingleRequest();
        googletag.pubads().setCentering(true);
        googletag.pubads().collapseEmptyDivs();
        googletag.pubads().disableInitialLoad();
        googletag.enableServices();
  
        googletag.pubads().addEventListener('slotOnload', function (event) {
          var adElement = document.getElementById(event.slot.getSlotElementId());
          if (adElement != null) {
            var $adElement = $(adElement);
            var localStorageId = 'ads.dfp.cache.dn.' + $adElement.attr('id') + ".height";
            var savedHeight = localStorage.getItem(localStorageId);
  
            setTimeout(function () {
              adElement.style.minHeight = null;
            }, 300);
  
            setTimeout(function () {
              var adElementHeight = $adElement.height();
              if (savedHeight != adElementHeight) {
                localStorage.setItem(localStorageId, adElementHeight);
              }
            }, 2000);
          }
        });
  
        googletag.pubads().addEventListener('slotRenderEnded', function (event) {
          if (event.slot.getSlotElementId() == "googlead-inline-artikkelbanner-first") {
            if (!event.isEmpty) {
              $('.inline-ad label.inline-ad__label').removeClass('is-hidden');
            }
          }
        });
      });
      //}
    }
  
  </script>

<h2 class="edition-sidebar__title">Siste utgave</h2>
<div class="edition-sidebar__cover">
<a class="edition edition--title-above" href="https://www.dn.no/avis/utgaver/vis/?utgave=4101&amp;page=41">
<figure class="edition__figure">

                if (typeof window.scrollMonitor !== "undefined") {
        
                  var adElement = document.getElementById(contentId);
                  if (adElement != null) {
                    var $adElement = $(adElement);
                    var localStorageId = 'ads.dfp.cache.dn.' + contentId + ".height";
                    var savedHeight = localStorage.getItem(localStorageId);
        
                    if (savedHeight != null && "0" != savedHeight) {
                      adElement.style.minHeight = savedHeight + 'px';
                    }
        
                    var adTopPosition = $adElement.offset().top;
                    if (adTopPosition >= window.scrollY && adTopPosition < (window.scrollY + window.innerHeight)) {
                      refreshAd();
                    } else {
                      var elementWatcher = scrollMonitor.create(adElement, 300);
                      elementWatcher.one('enterViewport', refreshAd);
                    }
                  }
                } else {
                  refreshAd();
                }
        
              })();

<script type="text/javascript">
          function newForslag() {
            var footerForm = document.getElementsByClassName('site-footer__form');
            var greetingForm = document.getElementsByClassName('site-footer__gretting');
            var errorArea = document.getElementById('error-message');
            footerForm[0].classList.remove('is-hidden');
            greetingForm[0].classList.add('is-hidden');
            errorArea.classList.add('is-hidden');
            footerForm[0].reset();
          }
        
          function sendForslag() {
            var forslagText = document.getElementById('forslag_text');
            var forslagEmail = document.getElementById('forslag_email');
            var errorArea = document.getElementById('error-message');
            var greetingForm = document.getElementsByClassName('site-footer__gretting');
        
            if (forslagText.value && forslagEmail.value) {
              forslagText.setAttribute('aria-invalid', false);
              forslagEmail.setAttribute('aria-invalid', false);
              errorArea.children[1].innerHTML = '';
        
              $.ajax({
                type: 'POST',
                url: window.apiHosts.userPref + '/dn/service/mail/footer',
                data: JSON.stringify({from: forslagEmail.value, text: forslagText.value}),
                dataType: 'text',
                success: function (data) {
                  if (data === 'OK') {
                    errorArea.parentNode.parentNode.classList.add('is-hidden');
                    greetingForm[0].classList.remove('is-hidden')
                  } else {
                    errorArea.children[1].innerHTML = 'Vennligst oppgi en melding';
                  }
                },
                error: function (error) {
                  errorArea.children[1].innerHTML = 'Vennligst oppgi en melding';
                }
              });
            } else if (!forslagText.value || !forslagEmail.value) {
              errorArea.classList.remove('is-hidden');
              errorArea.children[1].innerHTML = 'Vennligst fyll inn alle feltene';
              forslagEmail.setAttribute('aria-invalid', false);
              forslagText.setAttribute('aria-invalid', false);
        
              if (!forslagText.value) {
                forslagText.setAttribute('aria-invalid', true);
              }
              if (!forslagEmail.value) {
                forslagEmail.setAttribute('aria-invalid', true);
              }
            }
          }
        </script> </div>

    for(var i = 0; i < headlines.length; i++) {
      var element = headlines[i];
      lineClamp(element, 2);
    }
  
    addTeaserLineClamp = function () {
      const teaserPreview = document.querySelectorAll('.teaser__preview');
  
      for(var i = 0; i < teaserPreview.length; i++) {
        var element = teaserPreview[i];
        if (navigator.userAgent.search("Firefox") >= 0) {
          lineClamp(element, 4);
        } else {
          lineClamp(element, 3);
        }
      }
  
    }
  
    fontSize = function () {
      $('.teaser__headline').textfill({
        maxFontPixels: 80,
        minFontPixels: 16,
        innerTag: 'a'
      });
    };
  
    if ($('.carousel--dn-investor__item-title').length) {
      $('.carousel--dn-investor__item-title').each(function (i, node) {
        n = calculateLineCount($(this));
  
        if (navigator.userAgent.search("Firefox") >= 0) {
          val = 1;
        } else {
          val = 0;
        }
  
        if (n === 1) {
          lineClamp(investorItemtext[i], 4 + val);
        } else if (n === 2) {
          lineClamp(investorItemtext[i], 3 + val);
        } else if (n === 3) {
          lineClamp(investorItemtext[i], 2 + val);
        } else if (n === 4) {
          lineClamp(investorItemtext[i], 1 + val);
        } else {
          lineClamp(investorItemtext[i], val);
        }
      });
    }
  
    addTeaserLineClamp();
    $(window).on('load', fontSize);
  </script>
